- **Miami Marlins (home) moneyline (-124, BetRivers)** -- [56ba4bb647b80640] event=4645b2d34e3503155b3ff308836621dc selection_id=9e8d61f45a38abf0 edge_bps=None p_model_provenance=none grade=A
  - because: Backing the home side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) starter_velocity_gap -- how far the opposing starter's fastball sits above league pace (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 3.64 mph, home 0.91 mph (this game's own values rest on: away over 268 measured fastballs; home over 167 measured fastballs); a gap of 2.74 mph, clearing the threshold of 1.96 mph (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 3,149 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at -1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism harms), so the signal points to the home side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter whose fastball sits above league pace is holding stuff the season line has not caught up to, so the lineup facing the harder thrower is the disadvantaged side; velocity leads results and the market prices results. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 56ba4bb647b80640.)

- **Toronto Blue Jays (away) moneyline (-199, Caesars (William Hill US))** -- [56ba4bb647b80640] event=56329e0195eb8df1d61a2ced249e623d selection_id=b5670c82188aefcf edge_bps=None p_model_provenance=none grade=A
  - because: Backing the away side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) starter_velocity_gap -- how far the opposing starter's fastball sits above league pace (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away -4.31 mph, home 1.50 mph (this game's own values rest on: away over 160 measured fastballs; home over 217 measured fastballs); a gap of 5.80 mph, clearing the threshold of 1.96 mph (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 3,149 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at -1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism harms), so the signal points to the away side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter whose fastball sits above league pace is holding stuff the season line has not caught up to, so the lineup facing the harder thrower is the disadvantaged side; velocity leads results and the market prices results. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 56ba4bb647b80640.)

- **San Francisco Giants (home) moneyline (-128, Caesars (William Hill US))** -- [ed2b9ec19d3b9b9a] event=7e08f8c46ae533c3c09d2e5d1a37ac2b selection_id=9e8d61f45a38abf0 edge_bps=None p_model_provenance=none grade=A
  - because: Backing the home side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) top_minus_bottom -- this lineup's top-of-order minus bottom-of-order strength (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 0.039 wOBA, home 0.062 wOBA (this game's own values rest on: away over 2,531 plate-appearance denominators; home over 2,434 plate-appearance denominators); a gap of 0.023 wOBA, clearing the threshold of 0.023 wOBA (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 4,838 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at +1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism favours), so the signal points to the home side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a top-heavy order concentrates its best bats where the extra plate appearances go, and club-level pricing averages that concentration away; the weakest mechanism of the six, kept because V4 stated it before seeing its result. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome ed2b9ec19d3b9b9a.)

- **Cincinnati Reds (away) moneyline (+160, Caesars (William Hill US))** -- [56ba4bb647b80640] event=a58a90b8a19f48d680546cac451b77ec selection_id=b5670c82188aefcf edge_bps=None p_model_provenance=none grade=A
  - because: Backing the away side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) starter_velocity_gap -- how far the opposing starter's fastball sits above league pace (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 0.15 mph, home 3.37 mph (this game's own values rest on: away over 177 measured fastballs; home over 247 measured fastballs); a gap of 3.22 mph, clearing the threshold of 1.96 mph (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 3,149 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at -1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism harms), so the signal points to the away side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter whose fastball sits above league pace is holding stuff the season line has not caught up to, so the lineup facing the harder thrower is the disadvantaged side; velocity leads results and the market prices results. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. It is published as a calibration reference -- how good the market's own number is -- never as a reason to bet.
